- **windows**: Windows-specific problems in development tools. Use when a tool reads or writes text on Windows, when mise shims fail, when a task's command runs only partly, when a tool links a directory, when long paths, PowerShell quoting, redirection or a persistent terminal misbehave, when arguments after `--` are lost, or when a toolchain's PATH order matters. ([nebularis/vyvid-tools](https://github.com/nebularis/vyvid-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/windows))

- **windows**: Windows OS administration including PowerShell 7 scripting, WSL2 setup and interop, Windows Server roles (AD, IIS), Task Scheduler, Windows Defender firewall, winget package management, registry, services, event logs, remote management (WinRM/SSH), and disk management. Activate for any Windows administration, PowerShell scripting, Windows Server setup, or Windows hardening task. ([Raidan-Ai/opencode-settings](https://github.com/Raidan-Ai/opencode-settings/tree/HEAD/skills/opencode/windows))
- **windows**: Tile the windows a session is working with before capturing anything, so screenshots show the work instead of one corner of it. Use whenever a session opens a window, drives two or more windows at once, takes a screenshot to check its own work or show the owner, or when a window is "missing", maximized, overlapping, off-screen, or the desktop layout looks wrong. ([SyntheticPrometheus/synthetic-prometheus](https://github.com/SyntheticPrometheus/synthetic-prometheus/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/windows))
- **windows**: Execute Windows VM operations through Umbral Worker tasks: Power Automate Desktop flows, interactive worker bootstrap, firewall setup, and filesystem read/write helpers under policy-controlled paths.
